For issues that are significant enough to \\\n166\twarrant a fix, propose a bead with:\n167\t- title: concise description of the issue\n168\t- body: what needs to be fixed and how\n169\t- priority: based on severity (1=critical, 2=important, 3=minor)\n170\t\n171\tDo not propose beads that duplicate any existing open beads listed above.\n172\tIf no significant issues are found, respond with: NO_ISSUES\";\n173\t\n174\t/// Built-in split template for auto-splitting beads with too many consecutive failures.\n175\t///\n176\t/// This template instructs the agent to decompose a problematic bead into\n177\t/// smaller, focused child beads and convert the parent into an umbrella.\n178\tconst DEFAULT_SPLIT_TEMPLATE: &str = \"\\\n179\t## Auto-Split: Decompose This Bead\n180\t\n181\tThis bead has failed {failure_count} times in a row and needs to be broken down \\\n182\tinto smaller, manageable pieces.\n183\t\n184\t### Bead to Split\n185\t\n186\t**Title:** {bead_title}\n187\t**Bead ID:** {bead_id}\n188\t**Description:**\n189\t{bead_body}\n190\t\n191\t### Your Task\n192\t\n193\tYou MUST split this bead into 3-5 smaller child beads. The parent is too big \\\n194\tor complex and has failed repeatedly. Your job is to decompose it into a \\\n195\tchain of focused, independently achievable tasks.\n196\t\n197\t### Requirements\n198\t\n199\t1. **Create 3-5 child beads** using the `br create` command:\n200\t   ```bash\n201\t   br create \\\"Child Title\\\" \\\"Child description and acceptance criteria\\\"\n202\t   ```\n203\t\n204\t2. **Each child must:**\n205\t   - Be independently completable and closable\n206\t   - Have a clear, focused scope\n207\t   - Start with a verb (e.g., \\\"Add\\\", \\\"Fix\\\", \\\"Implement\\\", \\\"Write\\\")\n208\t   - Include acceptance criteria in the body\n209\t   - Be added with label: `split-child`\n210\t\n211\t3. **Chain the children sequentially:**\n212\t   - Child 2 depends on Child 1\n213\t   - Child 3 depends on Child 2\n214\t   - (Continue for all children)\n215\t\n216\t4. **Convert this parent into an umbrella:**\n217\t   - Add a dependency: this parent depends on the LAST child\n218\t   - Add label: `umbrella` to this parent\n219\t\n220\t5. **Verify the setup:**\n221\t   ```bash\n222\t   br show {bead_id}  # Should show umbrella label and dependency on last child\n223\t   br show <last-child-id>  # Should show this parent as a dependent\n224\t   ```\n225\t\n226\t### Example\n227\t\n228\tIf splitting \\\"Build authentication system with OAuth and JWT\\\":\n229\t1. Create child: \\\"Design OAuth2 flow\\\" with label split-child\n230\t2. Create child: \\\"Implement JWT token service\\\" with label split-child, depends on child 1\n231\t3. Create child: \\\"Add OAuth2 login endpoint\\\" with label split-child, depends on child 2\n232\t4. Parent depends on child 3 and gets umbrella label\n233\t\n234\t### Output Format\n235\t\n236\tAfter creating all children and wiring dependencies, output:\n237\t```\n238\tSPLIT_COMPLETE: Created N children, parent converted to umbrella\n239\t```\n240\t\n241\tList the created child IDs:\n242\t```\n243\tChildren: needle-xxx, needle-yyy, needle-zzz\n244\t```\n245\t\n246\tDo NOT close this bead. Just verify the split is complete and output the \\\n247\tSPLIT_COMPLETE message.\";\n248\t\n249\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n250\t// Known template names and their allowed variables\n251\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n252\t\n253\t/// Common variables available to all templates.\n254\tconst COMMON_VARS: &[&str] = &[\n255\t    \"{bead_id}\",\n256\t    \"{bead_title}\",\n257\t    \"{bead_body}\",\n258\t    \"{workspace_path}\",\n259\t    \"{context_file_contents}\",\n260\t    \"{workspace_instructions}\",\n261\t    \"{worker_id}\",\n262\t];\n263\t\n264\t/// Returns the extra (strand-specific) variables allowed for a given template name.\n265\t/// Returns `None` for unknown template names.\n266\tfn extra_vars_for_template(name: &str) -> Option<&'static [&'static str]> {\n267\t    match name {\n268\t        \"pluck\" => Some(&[]),\n269\t        \"split\" => Some(&[\"{failure_count}\"]),\n270\t        \"mitosis\" => Some(&[\"{existing_children}\"]),\n271\t        \"weave\" => Some(&[\"{doc_files}\", \"{existing_beads}\"]),\n272\t        \"unravel\" => Some(&[\"{human_bead_context}\"]),\n273\t        \"pulse\" => Some(&[\"{scan_results}\", \"{existing_beads}\"]),\n274\t        _ => None,\n275\t    }\n276\t}\n277\t\n278\t/// All known built-in template names (used in tests to verify defaults).\n279\t#[cfg(test)]\n280\tconst KNOWN_TEMPLATE_NAMES: &[&str] = &[\"pluck\", \"split\", \"mitosis\", \"weave\", \"unravel\", \"pulse\"];\n281\t\n282\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n283\t// BuiltPrompt\n284\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n285\t\n286\t/// The rendered output of prompt construction.\n287\t#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n288\tpub struct BuiltPrompt {\n289\t    /// The fully rendered prompt string.\n290\t    pub content: String,\n291\t    /// SHA-256 hex digest of `content` (for telemetry reproducibility).\n292\t    pub hash: String,\n293\t    /// Rough token estimate (chars / 4).\n294\t    pub token_estimate: u64,\n295\t    /// Name of the template used to build this prompt (e.g., `\"pluck\"`).\n296\t    pub template_name: String,\n297\t    /// Version tag identifying which variant/version was used\n298\t    /// (e.g., `\"pluck-default\"`, `\"pluck-v2\"`).\n299\t    pub template_version: String,\n300\t}\n301\t\n302\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n303\t// PromptBuilder\n304\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n305\t\n306\t/// Constructs agent prompts from bead context.\n307\t///\n308\t/// All five named templates (`pluck`, `mitosis`, `weave`, `unravel`, `pulse`)\n309\t/// are always present with built-in defaults. User-provided overrides from\n310\t/// config replace specific templates while others keep their defaults.\n311\t#[derive(Clone)]\n312\tpub struct PromptBuilder {\n313\t    /// Named templates. All five built-in names are always present.\n314\t    templates: BTreeMap<String, String>,\n315\t    /// Paths to context files (relative to the workspace root).\n316\t    context_file_paths: Vec<std::path::PathBuf>,\n317\t    /// Free-form workspace instructions appended to prompts.\n318\t    workspace_instructions: Option<String>,\n319\t    /// Cached learnings content for the workspace (if exists).\n320\t    learnings_content: Option<String>,\n321\t    /// Cached global learnings content (cross-workspace patterns, if exists).\n322\t    global_learnings_content: Option<String>,\n323\t    /// Skill library for matching and injecting relevant skills into prompts.\n324\t    skill_library: Option<SkillLibrary>,\n325\t    /// A/B test variant configurations per template name.\n326\t    variants: BTreeMap<String, Vec<VariantConfig>>,\n327\t}\n328\t\n329\timpl PromptBuilder {\n330\t    /// Create a new `PromptBuilder` from prompt config.\n331\t    ///\n332\t    /// Initializes all built-in templates, then overrides any that are\n333\t    /// specified in `config.templates`.\n334\t    pub fn new(config: &PromptConfig) -> Self {\n335\t        let mut templates = BTreeMap::new();\n336\t        templates.insert(\"pluck\".to_string(), DEFAULT_PLUCK_TEMPLATE.to_string());\n337\t        templates.insert(\"split\".to_string(), DEFAULT_SPLIT_TEMPLATE.to_string());\n338\t        templates.insert(\"mitosis\".to_string(), DEFAULT_MITOSIS_TEMPLATE.to_string());\n339\t        templates.insert(\"weave\".to_string(), DEFAULT_WEAVE_TEMPLATE.to_string());\n340\t        templates.insert(\"unravel\".to_string(), DEFAULT_UNRAVEL_TEMPLATE.to_string());\n341\t        templates.insert(\"pulse\".to_string(), DEFAULT_PULSE_TEMPLATE.to_string());\n342\t\n343\t        // Apply user overrides (partial: only specified templates are replaced).\n344\t        for (name, body) in &config.templates {\n345\t            templates.insert(name.clone(), body.clone());\n346\t        }\n347\t\n348\t        PromptBuilder {\n349\t            templates,\n350\t            context_file_paths: config.context_files.clone(),\n351\t            workspace_instructions: config.instructions.clone(),\n352\t            learnings_content: None,\n353\t            global_learnings_content: None,\n354\t            skill_library: None,\n355\t            variants: config.variants.clone(),\n356\t        }\n357\t    }\n358\t\n359\t    /// Create a new `PromptBuilder` with workspace-specific learnings.\n360\t    ///\n361\t    /// This variant loads the `.beads/learnings.md` file if it exists,\n362\t    /// automatically injecting workspace learnings into all prompts.\n363\t    pub fn with_workspace(config: &PromptConfig, workspace: &Path) -> Result<Self> {\n364\t        let mut builder = Self::new(config);\n365\t\n366\t        // Load learnings if the file exists.\n367\t        let learnings = LearningsFile::load(workspace);\n368\t        if let Ok(learnings_file) = learnings {\n369\t            if !learnings_file.entries().is_empty() {\n370\t                builder.learnings_content = Some(learnings_file.to_prompt_content());\n371\t            }\n372\t        }\n373\t\n374\t        // Load skill library (missing directory is silently ignored).\n375\t        if let Ok(lib) = SkillLibrary::load(workspace) {\n376\t            if !lib.is_empty() {\n377\t                builder.skill_library = Some(lib);\n378\t            }\n379\t        }\n380\t\n381\t        Ok(builder)\n382\t    }\n383\t\n384\t    /// Load skills from additional workspaces whose skill labels match the given\n385\t    /// workspace labels, merging them with any locally-loaded skills.\n386\t    ///\n387\t    /// This implements cross-workspace skill sharing: a skill tagged `[rust, api]`\n388\t    /// in another workspace is made available here if this workspace's labels\n389\t    /// include `rust` or `api`. All skills (local + cross-workspace) are ranked\n390\t    /// together by `match_score` and `success_count` at prompt-build time.\n391\t    ///\n392\t    /// Silently ignores workspaces with no skills directory.\n393\t    pub fn with_cross_workspace_skills(\n394\t        mut self,\n395\t        workspaces: &[std::path::PathBuf],\n396\t        workspace_labels: &[String],\n397\t    ) -> Self {\n398\t        if workspace_labels.is_empty() || workspaces.is_empty() {\n399\t            return self;\n400\t        }\n401\t\n402\t        let mut lib = self\n403\t            .skill_library\n404\t            .take()\n405\t            .unwrap_or_else(crate::skill::SkillLibrary::new_empty);\n406\t\n407\t        for workspace in workspaces {\n408\t            lib.extend_from_workspace(workspace, workspace_labels);\n409\t        }\n410\t\n411\t        if !lib.is_empty() {\n412\t            self.skill_library = Some(lib);\n413\t        }\n414\t\n415\t        self\n416\t    }\n417\t\n418\t    /// Load global learnings from the given path into this builder.\n419\t    ///\n420\t    /// Global learnings are cross-workspace patterns promoted by the consolidator.\n421\t    /// They are injected after workspace learnings, before skills.\n422\t    /// Missing or empty files are silently ignored.\n423\t    pub fn with_global_learnings(mut self, path: &Path) -> Self {\n424\t        if let Ok(file) = GlobalLearningsFile::load(path) {\n425\t            let content = file.to_prompt_content();\n426\t            if !content.is_empty() {\n427\t                self.global_learnings_content = Some(content);\n428\t            }\n429\t        }\n430\t        self\n431\t    }\n432\t\n433\t    /// Validate all templates at boot time.\n434\t    ///\n435\t    /// For each known template, checks that every `{variable}` reference in the\n436\t    /// template body is a recognized variable. Returns an error listing all\n437\t    /// invalid references found.\n438\t    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<()> {\n439\t        let mut errors = Vec::new();\n440\t\n441\t        for (name, body) in &self.templates {\n442\t            let allowed_extra = extra_vars_for_template(name);\n443\t\n444\t            // Extract all {variable} references from the template body.\n445\t            // Skip escaped braces ({{ and }}) used for literal JSON.\n446\t            let vars = extract_template_vars(body);\n447\t\n448\t            for var in vars {\n449\t                let is_common = COMMON_VARS.contains(&var.as_str());\n450\t                let is_extra = allowed_extra\n451\t                    .map(|extras| extras.contains(&var.as_str()))\n452\t                    .unwrap_or(false);\n453\t\n454\t                if !is_common && !is_extra {\n455\t                    errors.push(format!(\"template \\\"{name}\\\": unknown variable {var}\"));\n456\t                }\n457\t            }\n458\t        }\n459\t\n460\t        if errors.is_empty() {\n461\t            Ok(())\n462\t        } else {\n463\t            bail!(\"invalid prompt template(s):\\n  {}\", errors.join(\"\\n  \"));\n464\t        }\n465\t    }\n466\t\n467\t    /// Build the prompt for a claimed bead using the named template.\n468\t    ///\n469\t    /// Common variables (`{bead_id}`, `{bead_title}`, etc.) are always substituted.\n470\t    /// Missing context files are silently omitted.\n471\t    pub fn build(\n472\t        &self,\n473\t        bead: &Bead,\n474\t        workspace: &Path,\n475\t        worker_id: &str,\n476\t        template_name: &str,\n477\t    ) -> Result<BuiltPrompt> {\n478\t        self.build_with_vars(bead, workspace, worker_id, template_name, &[])\n479\t    }\n480\t\n481\t    /// Build the prompt with additional strand-specific variables.\n482\t    ///\n483\t    /// `extra_vars` is a slice of `(variable_name, value)` pairs for\n484\t    /// strand-specific substitutions (e.g., `(\"{doc_files}\", \"...\")` for weave).\n485\t    #[tracing::instrument(\n486\t        name = \"bead.prompt_build\",\n487\t        skip(self, bead, workspace, extra_vars),\n488\t        fields(\n489\t            needle.bead.id = %bead.id,\n490\t            needle.prompt.template_name = %template_name,\n491\t            needle.prompt.template_version = tracing::field::Empty,\n492\t            needle.prompt.token_estimate = tracing::field::Empty,\n493\t        )\n494\t    )]\n495\t    pub fn build_with_vars(\n496\t        &self,\n497\t        bead: &Bead,\n498\t        workspace: &Path,\n499\t        worker_id: &str,\n500\t        template_name: &str,\n501\t        extra_vars: &[(&str, &str)],\n502\t    ) -> Result<BuiltPrompt> {\n503\t        // Resolve template: variant takes precedence over built-in.\n504\t        let (template_version, variant_content) =\n505\t            self.select_variant(worker_id, template_name, workspace);\n506\t        let template_content: &str = match variant_content {\n507\t            Some(ref c) => c.as_str(),\n508\t            None => self\n509\t                .templates\n510\t                .get(template_name)\n511\t                .with_context(|| format!(\"unknown prompt template: {template_name}\"))?\n512\t                .as_str(),\n513\t        };\n514\t\n515\t        // Build context section: files + learnings + matching skills (in that order).\n516\t        let mut context_parts = vec![self.load_context_files(workspace)];\n517\t        if let Some(ref lib) = self.skill_library {\n518\t            let matching = lib.matching_skills(&bead.labels, &bead.title);\n519\t            if !matching.is_empty() {\n520\t                let skill_content = SkillLibrary::to_prompt_content(&matching);\n521\t                context_parts.push(skill_content);\n522\t            }\n523\t        }\n524\t        let context_file_contents = context_parts\n525\t            .into_iter()\n526\t            .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())\n527\t            .collect::<Vec<_>>()\n528\t            .join(\"\\n\\n\");\n529\t\n530\t        let instructions = self\n531\t            .workspace_instructions\n532\t            .as_deref()\n533\t            .unwrap_or(\"(no workspace instructions)\");\n534\t        let body = bead.body.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"(no description)\");\n535\t\n536\t        // Substitute common variables.\n537\t        let mut content = template_content\n538\t            .replace(\"{bead_id}\", bead.id.as_ref())\n539\t            .replace(\"{bead_title}\", &bead.title)\n540\t            .replace(\"{bead_body}\", body)\n541\t            .replace(\"{workspace_path}\", &workspace.display().to_string())\n542\t            .replace(\"{context_file_contents}\", &context_file_contents)\n543\t            .replace(\"{workspace_instructions}\", instructions)\n544\t            .replace(\"{worker_id}\", worker_id);\n545\t\n546\t        // Substitute strand-specific variables.\n547\t        for (var, value) in extra_vars {\n548\t            content = content.replace(var, value);\n549\t        }\n550\t\n551\t        let hash = hex_sha256(&content);\n552\t        let token_estimate = content.len() as u64 / 4;\n553\t\n554\t        // Record span attributes\n555\t        tracing::Span::current().record(\"needle.prompt.template_version\", &template_version);\n556\t        tracing::Span::current().record(\"needle.prompt.token_estimate\", token_estimate);\n557\t\n558\t        Ok(BuiltPrompt {\n559\t            content,\n560\t            hash,\n561\t            token_estimate,\n562\t            template_name: template_name.to_string(),\n563\t            template_version,\n564\t        })\n565\t    }\n566\t\n567\t    /// Convenience method that uses the `\"pluck\"` template.\n568\t    pub fn build_pluck(\n569\t        &self,\n570\t        bead: &Bead,\n571\t        workspace: &Path,\n572\t        worker_id: &str,\n573\t    ) -> Result<BuiltPrompt> {\n574\t        self.build(bead, workspace, worker_id, \"pluck\")\n575\t    }\n576\t\n577\t    /// Build a split (auto-split) prompt.\n578\t    ///\n579\t    /// `failure_count` — the current consecutive failure count for the bead.\n580\t    pub fn build_split(\n581\t        &self,\n582\t        bead: &Bead,\n583\t        workspace: &Path,\n584\t        worker_id: &str,\n585\t        failure_count: u32,\n586\t    ) -> Result<BuiltPrompt> {\n587\t        self.build_with_vars(\n588\t            bead,\n589\t            workspace,\n590\t            worker_id,\n591\t            \"split\",\n592\t            &[(\"{failure_count}\", &failure_count.to_string())],\n593\t        )\n594\t    }\n595\t\n596\t    /// Build a weave (gap analysis) prompt.\n597\t    ///\n598\t    /// `doc_files` — formatted listing of documentation files and contents.\n599\t    /// `existing_beads` — formatted listing of current open beads.\n600\t    pub fn build_weave(\n601\t        &self,\n602\t        bead: &Bead,\n603\t        workspace: &Path,\n604\t        worker_id: &str,\n605\t        doc_files: &str,\n606\t        existing_beads: &str,\n607\t    ) -> Result<BuiltPrompt> {\n608\t        self.build_with_vars(\n609\t            bead,\n610\t            workspace,\n611\t            worker_id,\n612\t            \"weave\",\n613\t            &[\n614\t                (\"{doc_files}\", doc_files),\n615\t                (\"{existing_beads}\", existing_beads),\n616\t            ],\n617\t        )\n618\t    }\n619\t\n620\t    /// Build an unravel (alternative proposals) prompt.\n621\t    ///\n622\t    /// `human_bead_context` — context about the HUMAN-blocked bead being analyzed.\n623\t    pub fn build_unravel(\n624\t        &self,\n625\t        bead: &Bead,\n626\t        workspace: &Path,\n627\t        worker_id: &str,\n628\t        human_bead_context: &str,\n629\t &[(\"{human_bead_context}\", human_bead_context)],\n636\t        )\n637\t    }\n638\t\n639\t    /// Build a pulse (health scan) prompt.\n640\t    ///\n641\t    /// `scan_results` — output from configured scanners.\n642\t    /// `existing_beads` — formatted listing of current open beads.\n643\t    pub fn build_pulse(\n644\t        &self,\n645\t        bead: &Bead,\n646\t        workspace: &Path,\n647\t        worker_id: &str,\n648\t        scan_results: &str,\n649\t        existing_beads: &str,\n650\t    ) -> Result<BuiltPrompt> {\n651\t        self.build_with_vars(\n652\t            bead,\n653\t            workspace,\n654\t            worker_id,\n655\t            \"pulse\",\n656\t            &[\n657\t                (\"{scan_results}\", scan_results),\n658\t                (\"{existing_beads}\", existing_beads),\n659\t            ],\n660\t        )\n661\t    }\n662\t\n663\t    /// Build a mitosis (split analysis) prompt.\n664\t    ///\n665\t    /// `existing_children` — formatted listing of the parent's current children.\n666\t    pub fn build_mitosis(\n667\t        &self,\n668\t        bead: &Bead,\n669\t        workspace: &Path,\n670\t        worker_id: &str,\n671\t        existing_children: &str,\n672\t    ) -> Result<BuiltPrompt> {\n673\t        self.build_with_vars(\n674\t            bead,\n675\t            workspace,\n676\t            worker_id,\n677\t            \"mitosis\",\n678\t            &[(\"{existing_children}\", existing_children)],\n679\t        )\n680\t    }\n681\t\n682\t    /// Returns an iterator over all template names.\n683\t    pub fn template_names(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &str> {\n684\t        self.templates.keys().map(|s| s.as_str())\n685\t    }\n686\t\n687\t    /// Load and concatenate context files from the workspace.\n688\t    ///\n689\t    /// Each file is prefixed with a header showing the file path.\n690\t    /// Missing files are silently skipped.\n691\t    fn load_context_files(&self, workspace: &Path) -> String {\n692\t        let mut sections = Vec::new();\n693\t\n694\t        // Load configured context files\n695\t        for rel_path in &self.context_file_paths {\n696\t            let abs_path = workspace.join(rel_path);\n697\t            match std::fs::read_to_string(&abs_path) {\n698\t                Ok(contents) => {\n699\t                    sections.push(format!(\n700\t                        \"### {}\\n\\n{}\",\n701\t                        rel_path.display(),\n702\t                        contents.trim_end()\n703\t                    ));\n704\t                }\n705\t                Err(_) => {\n706\t                    // Silently omit missing files per spec.\n707\t                }\n708\t            }\n709\t        }\n710\t\n711\t        // Append workspace learnings if available\n712\t        if let Some(ref learnings) = self.learnings_content {\n713\t            sections.push(learnings.clone());\n714\t        }\n715\t\n716\t        // Append global learnings after workspace learnings (cross-workspace patterns)\n717\t        if let Some(ref global) = self.global_learnings_content {\n718\t            sections.push(global.clone());\n719\t        }\n720\t\n721\t        if sections.is_empty() {\n722\t            \"(no context files found)\".to_string()\n723\t        } else {\n724\t            sections.join(\"\\n\\n\")\n725\t        }\n726\t    }\n727\t\n728\t    /// Select the variant version for a given template and worker.\n729\t    ///\n730\t    /// Returns `(template_version, variant_content)`:\n731\t    /// - `template_version`: e.g. `\"pluck-default\"` or `\"pluck-v2\"`\n732\t    /// - `variant_content`: `Some(String)` if a variant file was loaded, `None` for the default\n733\t    ///\n734\t    /// Assignment is deterministic: `worker_bucket(worker_id)` in `[0, 99]` is compared\n735\t    /// against cumulative variant weights.  Same `worker_id` always produces the same result.\n736\t    fn select_variant(\n737\t        &self,\n738\t        worker_id: &str,\n739\t        template_name: &str,\n740\t        workspace: &Path,\n741\t    ) -> (String, Option<String>) {\n742\t        let Some(variants) = self.variants.get(template_name) else {\n743\t            return (format!(\"{template_name}-default\"), None);\n744\t        };\n745\t\n746\t        if variants.is_empty() {\n747\t            return (format!(\"{template_name}-default\"), None);\n748\t        }\n749\t\n750\t        let bucket = worker_bucket(worker_id);\n751\t        let mut cumulative: u32 = 0;\n752\t\n753\t        for variant in variants {\n754\t            cumulative += u32::from(variant.weight);\n755\t            if u32::from(bucket) < cumulative {\n756\t                let abs_path = workspace.join(&variant.content_file);\n757\t                match std::fs::read_to_string(&abs_path) {\n758\t                    Ok(content) => {\n759\t                        return (format!(\"{template_name}-{}\", variant.name), Some(content));\n760\t                    }\n761\t                    Err(_) => {\n762\t                        // Content file missing — fall back to the built-in default.\n763\t                        return (format!(\"{template_name}-default\"), None);\n764\t                    }\n765\t                }\n766\t            }\n767\t        }\n768\t\n769\t        // Cumulative weights < 100: worker falls outside all variant ranges.\n770\t        (format!(\"{template_name}-default\"), None)\n771\t    }\n772\t}\n773\t\n774\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n775\t// Helpers\n776\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n777\t\n778\t/// Compute the worker's variant bucket: `hash(worker_id) % 100` in `[0, 99]`.\n779\t///\n780\t/// Uses the first 8 bytes of the SHA-256 digest of `worker_id` as a `u64`,\n781\t/// then takes modulo 100.  The result is deterministic and stable.\n782\tfn worker_bucket(worker_id: &str) -> u8 {\n783\t    let mut hasher = Sha256::new();\n784\t    hasher.update(worker_id.as_bytes());\n785\t    let result = hasher.finalize();\n786\t    let n = u64::from_le_bytes(result[..8].try_into().expect(\"sha256 is at least 8 bytes\"));\n787\t    (n % 100) as u8\n788\t}\n789\t\n790\t/// Compute the SHA-256 hex digest of a string.\n791\tfn hex_sha256(s: &str) -> String {\n792\t    let mut hasher = Sha256::new();\n793\t    hasher.update(s.as_bytes());\n794\t    let result = hasher.finalize();\n795\t    // Format as lowercase hex\n796\t    result.iter().fold(String::with_capacity(64), |mut acc, b| {\n797\t        use std::fmt::Write;\n798\t        let _ = write!(acc, \"{b:02x}\");\n799\t        acc\n800\t    })\n801\t}\n802\t\n803\t/// Extract all `{variable}` references from a template string.\n804\t///\n805\t/// Skips escaped braces (`{{` and `}}`) which are used for literal JSON.\n806\t/// Returns unique variable references including the braces (e.g., `\"{bead_id}\"`).\n807\tfn extract_template_vars(template: &str) -> Vec<String> {\n808\t    let mut vars = Vec::new();\n809\t    let chars: Vec<char> = template.chars().collect();\n810\t    let len = chars.len();\n811\t    let mut i = 0;\n812\t\n813\t    while i < len {\n814\t        if chars[i] == '{' {\n815\t            // Skip escaped braces: {{\n816\t            if i + 1 < len && chars[i + 1] == '{' {\n817\t                i += 2;\n818\t                continue;\n819\t            }\n820\t            // Find matching closing brace.\n821\t            if let Some(end) = chars[i + 1..].iter().position(|&c| c == '}') {\n822\t                let end = i + 1 + end;\n823\t                let var_name: String = chars[i..=end].iter().collect();\n824\t                // Only include if it looks like a valid variable (alphanumeric + underscore).\n825\t                let inner: String = chars[i + 1..end].iter().collect();\n826\t                if !inner.is_empty()\n827\t                    && inner.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_')\n828\t                    && !vars.contains(&var_name)\n829\t                {\n830\t                    vars.push(var_name);\n831\t                }\n832\t                i = end + 1;\n833\t            } else {\n834\t                i += 1;\n835\t            }\n836\t        } else {\n837\t            i += 1;\n838\t        }\n839\t    }\n840\t\n841\t    vars\n842\t}\n843\t\n844\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n845\t// Tests\n846\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n847\t\n848\t#[cfg(test)]\n849\tmod tests {\n850\t    use super::*;\n851\t    use crate::config::PromptConfig;\n852\t {\n1262\t        let mut templates = BTreeMap::new();\n1263\t        templates.insert(\n1264\t            \"pluck\".to_string(),\n1265\t            \"Hello {bead_title} Prompt construction from bead context.\n//!\n//! `PromptBuilder` constructs a deterministic prompt string from a claimed bead.\n//! Same bead state + same config always produces the identical prompt, making\n//! prompt hashes useful for telemetry and reproducibility.\n//!\n//! All agent-invoking operations (Pluck, Mitosis, Weave, Unravel, Pulse) use\n//! named templates. Templates are configurable per-workspace and globally.\n//!\n//! Depends on: `types`, `config`.\n\nuse std::collections::BTreeMap;\nuse std::path::Path;\n\nuse anyhow::{bail, Context, Result};\nuse sha2::{Digest, Sha256};\n\nuse crate::config::{PromptConfig, VariantConfig};\nuse crate::learning::{GlobalLearningsFile, LearningsFile};\nuse crate::skill::SkillLibrary;\nuse crate::types::Bead;\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Default templates\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Built-in pluck template used when no override is configured.\nconst DEFAULT_PLUCK_TEMPLATE: &str = \"\\\n## Task\n\n{bead_title}\n\n## Description\n\n{bead_body}\n\n## Workspace\n\n{workspace_path}\n\n## Context Files\n\n{context_file_contents}\n\n## Instructions\n\n{workspace_instructions}\n\nComplete the task described above. When finished:\n- **Commit all work with `git commit` before closing.** Every completed bead MUST produce at least one commit. If your work produced no file changes, create `notes/{bead_id}.md` summarizing what you did and commit that file. Do not close the bead without committing.\n- **Push commits with `git push` after committing.** Always push to the remote after a successful commit.\n- Close the bead:\n\n`br close {bead_id}`\n\nIf you cannot complete the task OR cannot produce a commit:\n- Do NOT close the bead\n- The bead will be automatically released for retry\n\nBead ID: {bead_id}\";\n\n/// Built-in mitosis analysis template.\n///\n/// The agent receives the bead context and must output JSON describing whether\n/// the bead contains multiple independent tasks and, if so, proposed child beads.\nconst DEFAULT_MITOSIS_TEMPLATE: &str = \"\\\n## Mitosis Analysis\n\nAnalyze the following bead and determine if it describes MULTIPLE INDEPENDENT TASKS.\n\n### Bead\n\n**Title:** {bead_title}\n\n**Description:**\n{bead_body}\n\n**Bead ID:** {bead_id}\n\n### Existing Children\n\n{existing_children}\n\n### Instructions\n\nYou must output ONLY a JSON object (no markdown fencing, no explanation).\n\nIf the bead describes multiple independent tasks that can be worked on separately:\n{{\\\"splittable\\\": true, \\\"children\\\": [{{\\\"title\\\": \\\"Short task title\\\", \\\"body\\\": \\\"Task description and acceptance criteria\\\"}}, ...]}}\n\nIf the bead describes a single task (even if complex or long):\n{{\\\"splittable\\\": false}}\n\n### Rules for splitting\n\n- Split ONLY if the bead asks for MORE THAN ONE independent unit of work\n- Each child must be independently completable and closable\n- Valid split: \\\"add endpoint AND write migration AND update tests\\\" (three deliverables)\n- Invalid split: bead is long, bead failed, bead has many acceptance criteria for one task\n- Preserve the original acceptance criteria by distributing them to the appropriate child\n- Each child title should be concise and start with a verb\n- Do not propose children that duplicate any existing children listed above\";\n\n/// Built-in weave template for gap analysis and bead creation.\nconst DEFAULT_WEAVE_TEMPLATE: &str = \"\\\n## Workspace Documentation\n\n{doc_files}\n\n## Current Open Beads\n\n{existing_beads}\n\n## Question\n\nReview the documentation above. Identify gaps where documented features, \\\nAPIs, or workflows are incomplete, missing tests, or have no corresponding \\\nimplementation bead.\n\nFor each gap found, propose a bead with:\n- title: concise description of what's missing\n- body: what needs to be done to close the gap\n- priority: 1 (critical), 2 (important), or 3 (nice-to-have)\n\nDo not propose beads that duplicate any existing open beads listed above.\nIf no gaps are found, respond with: NO_GAPS\";\n\n/// Built-in unravel template for proposing alternatives to HUMAN-blocked beads.\nconst DEFAULT_UNRAVEL_TEMPLATE: &str = \"\\\n## Blocked Bead\n\nTitle: {bead_title}\nBody: {bead_body}\nStatus: Blocked (requires human decision)\n\n## Context\n\n{human_bead_context}\n\n## Question\n\nThis bead is blocked because it requires a human decision. Analyze the bead \\\nand propose alternative approaches that could be executed by an automated \\\nagent without the human decision.\n\nFor each alternative, provide:\n- title: concise description of the alternative approach\n- body: what would be done differently\n- tradeoffs: what is gained and what is lost compared to the original approach\n\nIf no viable alternatives exist, respond with: NO_ALTERNATIVES\";\n\n/// Built-in pulse template for health scan bead creation.\nconst DEFAULT_PULSE_TEMPLATE: &str = \"\\\n## Scan Results\n\n{scan_results}\n\n## Current Open Beads\n\n{existing_beads}\n\n## Question\n\nReview the scan results above. For issues that are significant enough to \\\nwarrant a fix, propose a bead with:\n- title: concise description of the issue\n- body: what needs to be fixed and how\n- priority: based on severity (1=critical, 2=important, 3=minor)\n\nDo not propose beads that duplicate any existing open beads listed above.\nIf no significant issues are found, respond with: NO_ISSUES\";\n\n/// Built-in split template for auto-splitting beads with too many consecutive failures.\n///\n/// This template instructs the agent to decompose a problematic bead into\n/// smaller, focused child beads and convert the parent into an umbrella.\nconst DEFAULT_SPLIT_TEMPLATE: &str = \"\\\n## Auto-Split: Decompose This Bead\n\nThis bead has failed {failure_count} times in a row and needs to be broken down \\\ninto smaller, manageable pieces.\n\n### Bead to Split\n\n**Title:** {bead_title}\n**Bead ID:** {bead_id}\n**Description:**\n{bead_body}\n\n### Your Task\n\nYou MUST split this bead into 3-5 smaller child beads. The parent is too big \\\nor complex and has failed repeatedly. Your job is to decompose it into a \\\nchain of focused, independently achievable tasks.\n\n### Requirements\n\n1. **Create 3-5 child beads** using the `br create` command:\n   ```bash\n   br create \\\"Child Title\\\" \\\"Child description and acceptance criteria\\\"\n   ```\n\n2. **Each child must:**\n   - Be independently completable and closable\n   - Have a clear, focused scope\n   - Start with a verb (e.g., \\\"Add\\\", \\\"Fix\\\", \\\"Implement\\\", \\\"Write\\\")\n   - Include acceptance criteria in the body\n   - Be added with label: `split-child`\n\n3. **Chain the children sequentially:**\n   - Child 2 depends on Child 1\n   - Child 3 depends on Child 2\n   - (Continue for all children)\n\n4. **Convert this parent into an umbrella:**\n   - Add a dependency: this parent depends on the LAST child\n   - Add label: `umbrella` to this parent\n\n5. **Verify the setup:**\n   ```bash\n   br show {bead_id}  # Should show umbrella label and dependency on last child\n   br show <last-child-id>  # Should show this parent as a dependent\n   ```\n\n### Example\n\nIf splitting \\\"Build authentication system with OAuth and JWT\\\":\n1. Create child: \\\"Design OAuth2 flow\\\" with label split-child\n2. Create child: \\\"Implement JWT token service\\\" with label split-child, depends on child 1\n3. Create child: \\\"Add OAuth2 login endpoint\\\" with label split-child, depends on child 2\n4. Parent depends on child 3 and gets umbrella label\n\n### Output Format\n\nAfter creating all children and wiring dependencies, output:\n```\nSPLIT_COMPLETE: Created N children, parent converted to umbrella\n```\n\nList the created child IDs:\n```\nChildren: needle-xxx, needle-yyy, needle-zzz\n```\n\nDo NOT close this bead. Just verify the split is complete and output the \\\nSPLIT_COMPLETE message.\";\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Known template names and their allowed variables\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Common variables available to all templates.\nconst COMMON_VARS: &[&str] = &[\n    \"{bead_id}\",\n    \"{bead_title}\",\n    \"{bead_body}\",\n    \"{workspace_path}\",\n    \"{context_file_contents}\",\n    \"{workspace_instructions}\",\n    \"{worker_id}\",\n];\n\n/// Returns the extra (strand-specific) variables allowed for a given template name.\n/// Returns `None` for unknown template names.\nfn extra_vars_for_template(name: &str) -> Option<&'static [&'static str]> {\n    match name {\n        \"pluck\" => Some(&[]),\n        \"split\" => Some(&[\"{failure_count}\"]),\n        \"mitosis\" => Some(&[\"{existing_children}\"]),\n        \"weave\" => Some(&[\"{doc_files}\", \"{existing_beads}\"]),\n        \"unravel\" => Some(&[\"{human_bead_context}\"]),\n        \"pulse\" => Some(&[\"{scan_results}\", \"{existing_beads}\"]),\n        _ => None,\n    }\n}\n\n/// All known built-in template names (used in tests to verify defaults).\n#[cfg(test)]\nconst KNOWN_TEMPLATE_NAMES: &[&str] = &[\"pluck\", \"split\", \"mitosis\", \"weave\", \"unravel\", \"pulse\"];\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// BuiltPrompt\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// The rendered output of prompt construction.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct BuiltPrompt {\n    /// The fully rendered prompt string.\n    pub content: String,\n    /// SHA-256 hex digest of `content` (for telemetry reproducibility).\n    pub hash: String,\n    /// Rough token estimate (chars / 4).\n    pub token_estimate: u64,\n    /// Name of the template used to build this prompt (e.g., `\"pluck\"`).\n    pub template_name: String,\n    /// Version tag identifying which variant/version was used\n    /// (e.g., `\"pluck-default\"`, `\"pluck-v2\"`).\n    pub template_version: String,\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// PromptBuilder\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Constructs agent prompts from bead context.\n///\n/// All five named templates (`pluck`, `mitosis`, `weave`, `unravel`, `pulse`)\n/// are always present with built-in defaults. User-provided overrides from\n/// config replace specific templates while others keep their defaults.\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct PromptBuilder {\n    /// Named templates. All five built-in names are always present.\n    templates: BTreeMap<String, String>,\n    /// Paths to context files (relative to the workspace root).\n    context_file_paths: Vec<std::path::PathBuf>,\n    /// Free-form workspace instructions appended to prompts.\n    workspace_instructions: Option<String>,\n    /// Cached learnings content for the workspace (if exists).\n    learnings_content: Option<String>,\n    /// Cached global learnings content (cross-workspace patterns, if exists).\n    global_learnings_content: Option<String>,\n    /// Skill library for matching and injecting relevant skills into prompts.\n    skill_library: Option<SkillLibrary>,\n    /// A/B test variant configurations per template name.\n    variants: BTreeMap<String, Vec<VariantConfig>>,\n}\n\nimpl PromptBuilder {\n    /// Create a new `PromptBuilder` from prompt config.\n    ///\n    /// Initializes all built-in templates, then overrides any that are\n    /// specified in `config.templates`.\n    pub fn new(config: &PromptConfig) -> Self {\n        let mut templates = BTreeMap::new();\n        templates.insert(\"pluck\".to_string(), DEFAULT_PLUCK_TEMPLATE.to_string());\n        templates.insert(\"split\".to_string(), DEFAULT_SPLIT_TEMPLATE.to_string());\n        templates.insert(\"mitosis\".to_string(), DEFAULT_MITOSIS_TEMPLATE.to_string());\n        templates.insert(\"weave\".to_string(), DEFAULT_WEAVE_TEMPLATE.to_string());\n        templates.insert(\"unravel\".to_string(), DEFAULT_UNRAVEL_TEMPLATE.to_string());\n        templates.insert(\"pulse\".to_string(), DEFAULT_PULSE_TEMPLATE.to_string());\n\n        // Apply user overrides (partial: only specified templates are replaced).\n        for (name, body) in &config.templates {\n            templates.insert(name.clone(), body.clone());\n        }\n\n        PromptBuilder {\n            templates,\n            context_file_paths: config.context_files.clone(),\n            workspace_instructions: config.instructions.clone(),\n            learnings_content: None,\n            global_learnings_content: None,\n            skill_library: None,\n            variants: config.variants.clone(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Create a new `PromptBuilder` with workspace-specific learnings.\n    ///\n    /// This variant loads the `.beads/learnings.md` file if it exists,\n    /// automatically injecting workspace learnings into all prompts.\n    pub fn with_workspace(config: &PromptConfig, workspace: &Path) -> Result<Self> {\n        let mut builder = Self::new(config);\n\n        // Load learnings if the file exists.\n        let learnings = LearningsFile::load(workspace);\n        if let Ok(learnings_file) = learnings {\n            if !learnings_file.entries().is_empty() {\n                builder.learnings_content = Some(learnings_file.to_prompt_content());\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Load skill library (missing directory is silently ignored).\n        if let Ok(lib) = SkillLibrary::load(workspace) {\n            if !lib.is_empty() {\n                builder.skill_library = Some(lib);\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(builder)\n    }\n\n    /// Load skills from additional workspaces whose skill labels match the given\n    /// workspace labels, merging them with any locally-loaded skills.\n    ///\n    /// This implements cross-workspace skill sharing: a skill tagged `[rust, api]`\n    /// in another workspace is made available here if this workspace's labels\n    /// include `rust` or `api`. All skills (local + cross-workspace) are ranked\n    /// together by `match_score` and `success_count` at prompt-build time.\n    ///\n    /// Silently ignores workspaces with no skills directory.\n    pub fn with_cross_workspace_skills(\n        mut self,\n        workspaces: &[std::path::PathBuf],\n        workspace_labels: &[String],\n    ) -> Self {\n        if workspace_labels.is_empty() || workspaces.is_empty() {\n            return self;\n        }\n\n        let mut lib = self\n            .skill_library\n            .take()\n            .unwrap_or_else(crate::skill::SkillLibrary::new_empty);\n\n        for workspace in workspaces {\n            lib.extend_from_workspace(workspace, workspace_labels);\n        }\n\n        if !lib.is_empty() {\n            self.skill_library = Some(lib);\n        }\n\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Load global learnings from the given path into this builder.\n    ///\n    /// Global learnings are cross-workspace patterns promoted by the consolidator.\n    /// They are injected after workspace learnings, before skills.\n    /// Missing or empty files are silently ignored.\n    pub fn with_global_learnings(mut self, path: &Path) -> Self {\n        if let Ok(file) = GlobalLearningsFile::load(path) {\n            let content = file.to_prompt_content();\n            if !content.is_empty() {\n                self.global_learnings_content = Some(content);\n            }\n        }\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Validate all templates at boot time.\n    ///\n    /// For each known template, checks that every `{variable}` reference in the\n    /// template body is a recognized variable. Returns an error listing all\n    /// invalid references found.\n    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<()> {\n        let mut errors = Vec::new();\n\n        for (name, body) in &self.templates {\n            let allowed_extra = extra_vars_for_template(name);\n\n            // Extract all {variable} references from the template body.\n            // Skip escaped braces ({{ and }}) used for literal JSON.\n            let vars = extract_template_vars(body);\n\n            for var in vars {\n                let is_common = COMMON_VARS.contains(&var.as_str());\n                let is_extra = allowed_extra\n                    .map(|extras| extras.contains(&var.as_str()))\n                    .unwrap_or(false);\n\n                if !is_common && !is_extra {\n                    errors.push(format!(\"template \\\"{name}\\\": unknown variable {var}\"));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        if errors.is_empty() {\n            Ok(())\n        } else {\n            bail!(\"invalid prompt template(s):\\n  {}\", errors.join(\"\\n  \"));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Build the prompt for a claimed bead using the named template.\n    ///\n    /// Common variables (`{bead_id}`, `{bead_title}`, etc.) are always substituted.\n    /// Missing context files are silently omitted.\n    pub fn build(\n        &self,\n        bead: &Bead,\n        workspace: &Path,\n        worker_id: &str,\n        template_name: &str,\n    ) -> Result<BuiltPrompt> {\n        self.build_with_vars(bead, workspace, worker_id, template_name, &[])\n    }\n\n    /// Build the prompt with additional strand-specific variables.\n    ///\n    /// `extra_vars` is a slice of `(variable_name, value)` pairs for\n    /// strand-specific substitutions (e.g., `(\"{doc_files}\", \"...\")` for weave).\n    #[tracing::instrument(\n        name = \"bead.prompt_build\",\n        skip(self, bead, workspace, extra_vars),\n        fields(\n            needle.bead.id = %bead.id,\n            needle.prompt.template_name = %template_name,\n            needle.prompt.template_version = tracing::field::Empty,\n            needle.prompt.token_estimate = tracing::field::Empty,\n        )\n    )]\n    pub fn build_with_vars(\n        &self,\n        bead: &Bead,\n        workspace: &Path,\n        worker_id: &str,\n        template_name: &str,\n        extra_vars: &[(&str, &str)],\n    ) -> Result<BuiltPrompt> {\n        // Resolve template: variant takes precedence over built-in.\n        let (template_version, variant_content) =\n            self.select_variant(worker_id, template_name, workspace);\n        let template_content: &str = match variant_content {\n            Some(ref c) => c.as_str(),\n            None => self\n                .templates\n                .get(template_name)\n                .with_context(|| format!(\"unknown prompt template: {template_name}\"))?\n                .as_str(),\n        };\n\n        // Build context section: files + learnings + matching skills (in that order).\n        let mut context_parts = vec![self.load_context_files(workspace)];\n        if let Some(ref lib) = self.skill_library {\n            let matching = lib.matching_skills(&bead.labels, &bead.title);\n            if !matching.is_empty() {\n                let skill_content = SkillLibrary::to_prompt_content(&matching);\n                context_parts.push(skill_content);\n            }\n        }\n        let context_file_contents = context_parts\n            .into_iter()\n            .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())\n            .collect::<Vec<_>>()\n            .join(\"\\n\\n\");\n\n        let instructions = self\n            .workspace_instructions\n            .as_deref()\n            .unwrap_or(\"(no workspace instructions)\");\n        let body = bead.body.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"(no description)\");\n\n        // Substitute common variables.\n        let mut content = template_content\n            .replace(\"{bead_id}\", bead.id.as_ref())\n            .replace(\"{bead_title}\", &bead.title)\n            .replace(\"{bead_body}\", body)\n            .replace(\"{workspace_path}\", &workspace.display().to_string())\n            .replace(\"{context_file_contents}\", &context_file_contents)\n            .replace(\"{workspace_instructions}\", instructions)\n            .replace(\"{worker_id}\", worker_id);\n\n        // Substitute strand-specific variables.\n        for (var, value) in extra_vars {\n            content = content.replace(var, value);\n        }\n\n        let hash = hex_sha256(&content);\n        let token_estimate = content.len() as u64 / 4;\n\n        // Record span attributes\n        tracing::Span::current().record(\"needle.prompt.template_version\", &template_version);\n        tracing::Span::current().record(\"needle.prompt.token_estimate\", token_estimate);\n\n        Ok(BuiltPrompt {\n            content,\n            hash,\n            token_estimate,\n            template_name: template_name.to_string(),\n            template_version,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Convenience method that uses the `\"pluck\"` template.\n    pub fn build_pluck(\n        &self,\n        bead: &Bead,\n        workspace: &Path,\n        worker_id: &str,\n    ) -> Result<BuiltPrompt> {\n        self.build(bead, workspace, worker_id, \"pluck\")\n    }\n\n    /// Build a split (auto-split) prompt.\n    ///\n    /// `failure_count` — the current consecutive failure count for the bead.\n    pub fn build_split(\n        &self,\n        bead: &Bead,\n        workspace: &Path,\n        worker_id: &str,\n        failure_count: u32,\n    ) -> Result<BuiltPrompt> {\n        self.build_with_vars(\n            bead,\n            workspace,\n            worker_id,\n            \"split\",\n            &[(\"{failure_count}\", &failure_count.to_string())],\n        )\n    }\n\n    /// Build a weave (gap analysis) prompt.\n    ///\n    /// `doc_files` — formatted listing of documentation files and contents.\n    /// `existing_beads` — formatted listing of current open beads.\n    pub fn build_weave(\n        &self,\n        bead: &Bead,\n        workspace: &Path,\n        worker_id: &str,\n        doc_files: &str,\n        existing_beads: &str,\n    ) -> Result<BuiltPrompt> {\n        self.build_with_vars(\n            bead,\n            workspace,\n            worker_id,\n            \"weave\",\n            &[\n                (\"{doc_files}\", doc_files),\n                (\"{existing_beads}\", existing_beads),\n            ],\n        )\n    }\n\n    /// Build an unravel (alternative proposals) prompt.\n    ///\n    /// `human_bead_context` — context about the HUMAN-blocked bead being analyzed.\n    pub fn build_unravel(\n        &self,\n        bead: &Bead,\n        workspace: &Path,\n        worker_id: &str,\n        human_bead_context: &str,\n    ) -> Result<BuiltPrompt> {\n        self.build_with_vars(\n            bead,\n            workspace,\n            worker_id,\n            \"unravel\",\n            &[(\"{human_bead_context}\", human_bead_context)],\n        )\n    }\n\n    /// Build a pulse (health scan) prompt.\n    ///\n    /// `scan_results` — output from configured scanners.\n    /// `existing_beads` — formatted listing of current open beads.\n    pub fn build_pulse(\n        &self,\n        bead: &Bead,\n        workspace: &Path,\n        worker_id: &str,\n        scan_results: &str,\n        existing_beads: &str,\n    ) -> Result<BuiltPrompt> {\n        self.build_with_vars(\n            bead,\n            workspace,\n            worker_id,\n            \"pulse\",\n            &[\n                (\"{scan_results}\", scan_results),\n                (\"{existing_beads}\", existing_beads),\n            ],\n        )\n    }\n\n    /// Build a mitosis (split analysis) prompt.\n    ///\n    /// `existing_children` — formatted listing of the parent's current children.\n    pub fn build_mitosis(\n        &self,\n        bead: &Bead,\n        workspace: &Path,\n        worker_id: &str,\n        existing_children: &str,\n    ) -> Result<BuiltPrompt> {\n        self.build_with_vars(\n            bead,\n            workspace,\n            worker_id,\n            \"mitosis\",\n            &[(\"{existing_children}\", existing_children)],\n        )\n    }\n\n    /// Returns an iterator over all template names.\n    pub fn template_names(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &str> {\n        self.templates.keys().map(|s| s.as_str())\n    }\n\n    /// Load and concatenate context files from the workspace.\n    ///\n    /// Each file is prefixed with a header showing the file path.\n    /// Missing files are silently skipped.\n    fn load_context_files(&self, workspace: &Path) -> String {\n        let mut sections = Vec::new();\n\n        // Load configured context files\n        for rel_path in &self.context_file_paths {\n            let abs_path = workspace.join(rel_path);\n            match std::fs::read_to_string(&abs_path) {\n                Ok(contents) => {\n                    sections.push(format!(\n                        \"### {}\\n\\n{}\",\n                        rel_path.display(),\n                        contents.trim_end()\n                    ));\n                }\n                Err(_) => {\n                    // Silently omit missing files per spec.\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Append workspace learnings if available\n        if let Some(ref learnings) = self.learnings_content {\n            sections.push(learnings.clone());\n        }\n\n        // Append global learnings after workspace learnings (cross-workspace patterns)\n        if let Some(ref global) = self.global_learnings_content {\n            sections.push(global.clone());\n        }\n\n        if sections.is_empty() {\n            \"(no context files found)\".to_string()\n        } else {\n            sections.join(\"\\n\\n\")\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Select the variant version for a given template and worker.\n    ///\n    /// Returns `(template_version, variant_content)`:\n    /// - `template_version`: e.g. `\"pluck-default\"` or `\"pluck-v2\"`\n    /// - `variant_content`: `Some(String)` if a variant file was loaded, `None` for the default\n    ///\n    /// Assignment is deterministic: `worker_bucket(worker_id)` in `[0, 99]` is compared\n    /// against cumulative variant weights.  Same `worker_id` always produces the same result.\n    fn select_variant(\n        &self,\n        worker_id: &str,\n        template_name: &str,\n        workspace: &Path,\n    ) -> (String, Option<String>) {\n        let Some(variants) = self.variants.get(template_name) else {\n            return (format!(\"{template_name}-default\"), None);\n        };\n\n        if variants.is_empty() {\n            return (format!(\"{template_name}-default\"), None);\n        }\n\n        let bucket = worker_bucket(worker_id);\n        let mut cumulative: u32 = 0;\n\n        for variant in variants {\n            cumulative += u32::from(variant.weight);\n            if u32::from(bucket) < cumulative {\n                let abs_path = workspace.join(&variant.content_file);\n                match std::fs::read_to_string(&abs_path) {\n                    Ok(content) => {\n                        return (format!(\"{template_name}-{}\", variant.name), Some(content));\n                    }\n                    Err(_) => {\n                        // Content file missing — fall back to the built-in default.\n                        return (format!(\"{template_name}-default\"), None);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Cumulative weights < 100: worker falls outside all variant ranges.\n        (format!(\"{template_name}-default\"), None)\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Helpers\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Compute the worker's variant bucket: `hash(worker_id) % 100` in `[0, 99]`.\n///\n/// Uses the first 8 bytes of the SHA-256 digest of `worker_id` as a `u64`,\n/// then takes modulo 100.  The result is deterministic and stable.\nfn worker_bucket(worker_id: &str) -> u8 {\n    let mut hasher = Sha256::new();\n    hasher.update(worker_id.as_bytes());\n    let result = hasher.finalize();\n    let n = u64::from_le_bytes(result[..8].try_into().expect(\"sha256 is at least 8 bytes\"));\n    (n % 100) as u8\n}\n\n/// Compute the SHA-256 hex digest of a string.\nfn hex_sha256(s: &str) -> String {\n    let mut hasher = Sha256::new();\n    hasher.update(s.as_bytes());\n    let result = hasher.finalize();\n    // Format as lowercase hex\n    result.iter().fold(String::with_capacity(64), |mut acc, b| {\n        use std::fmt::Write;\n        let _ = write!(acc, \"{b:02x}\");\n        acc\n    })\n}\n\n/// Extract all `{variable}` references from a template string.\n///\n/// Skips escaped braces (`{{` and `}}`) which are used for literal JSON.\n/// Returns unique variable references including the braces (e.g., `\"{bead_id}\"`).\nfn extract_template_vars(template: &str) -> Vec<String> {\n    let mut vars = Vec::new();\n    let chars: Vec<char> = template.chars().collect();\n    let len = chars.len();\n    let mut i = 0;\n\n    while i < len {\n        if chars[i] == '{' {\n            // Skip escaped braces: {{\n            if i + 1 < len && chars[i + 1] == '{' {\n                i += 2;\n                continue;\n            }\n            // Find matching closing brace.\n            if let Some(end) = chars[i + 1..].iter().position(|&c| c == '}') {\n                let end = i + 1 + end;\n                let var_name: String = chars[i..=end].iter().collect();\n                // Only include if it looks like a valid variable (alphanumeric + underscore).\n                let inner: String = chars[i + 1..end].iter().collect();\n                if !inner.is_empty()\n                    && inner.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_')\n                    && !vars.contains(&var_name)\n                {\n                    vars.push(var_name);\n                }\n                i = end + 1;\n            } else {\n                i += 1;\n            }\n        } else {\n            i += 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    vars\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Tests\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n31\t\n32\t/// Raw output from an agent process execution.\n33\t#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n34\tpub struct ExecutionResult {\n35\t    /// Process exit code (124 if killed by timeout).\n36\t    pub exit_code: i32,\n37\t    /// Captured stdout.\n38\t    pub stdout: String,\n39\t    /// Captured stderr.\n40\t    pub stderr: String,\n41\t    /// Wall-clock time from spawn to exit.\n42\t    pub elapsed: Duration,\n43\t    /// OS process ID.\n44\t    pub pid: u32,\n45\t    /// Path to trace directory if trace capture was enabled.\n46\t    pub trace_path: Option<std::path::PathBuf>,\n47\t}\n48\t\n49\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n50\t// Token extraction\n51\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n52\t\n53\t/// How to extract token usage from agent output.\n54\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]\n55\t#[serde(tag = \"method\", rename_all = \"snake_case\")]\n56\tpub enum TokenExtraction {\n57\t    /// Extract from JSON fields in stdout (e.g., Claude Code --output-format json).\n58\t    JsonField {\n59\t        /// JSON path for input tokens (e.g., `result.usage.input_tokens`).\n60\t        input_path: String,\n61\t        /// JSON path for output tokens (e.g., `result.usage.output_tokens`).\n62\t        output_path: String,\n63\t    },\n64\t    /// Extract from stdout/stderr using a regex with capture groups.\n65\t    Regex {\n66\t        /// Regex pattern with capture groups for token counts.\n67\t        pattern: String,\n68\t        /// 1-based capture group index for input tokens.\n69\t        input_group: usize,\n70\t        /// 1-based capture group index for output tokens.\n71\t        output_group: usize,\n72\t    },\n73\t    /// No token extraction.\n74\t    #[default]\n75\t    None,\n76\t}\n77\t\n78\t/// Extracted token usage from agent output.\n79\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]\n80\tpub struct TokenUsage {\n81\t    /// Input tokens consumed (None if extraction failed or not configured).\n82\t    pub input_tokens: Option<u64>,\n83\t    /// Output tokens produced (None if extraction failed or not configured).\n84\t    pub output_tokens: Option<u64>,\n85\t}\n86\t\n87\t/// Extract token usage from agent output using the configured method.\n88\tpub fn extract_tokens(extraction: &TokenExtraction, stdout: &str, stderr: &str) -> TokenUsage {\n89\t    match extraction {\n90\t        TokenExtraction::None => TokenUsage::default(),\n91\t        TokenExtraction::JsonField {\n92\t            input_path,\n93\t            output_path,\n94\t        } => extract_tokens_json(stdout, input_path, output_path),\n95\t        TokenExtraction::Regex {\n96\t            pattern,\n97\t            input_group,\n98\t            output_group,\n99\t        } => {\n100\t            // Search both stdout and stderr for the pattern.\n101\t            let combined = format!(\"{stdout}\\n{stderr}\");\n102\t            extract_tokens_regex(&combined, pattern, *input_group, *output_group)\n103\t        }\n104\t    }\n105\t}\n106\t\n107\t/// Extract tokens from JSON output using dot-separated path notation.\n108\tfn extract_tokens_json(stdout: &str, input_path: &str, output_path: &str) -> TokenUsage {\n109\t    let parsed: serde_json::Value = match serde_json::from_str(stdout) {\n110\t        Ok(v) => v,\n111\t        Err(_) => return TokenUsage::default(),\n112\t    };\n113\t\n114\t    let input_tokens = resolve_json_path(&parsed, input_path).and_then(|v| v.as_u64());\n115\t    let output_tokens = resolve_json_path(&parsed, output_path).and_then(|v| v.as_u64());\n116\t\n117\t    TokenUsage {\n118\t        input_tokens,\n119\t        output_tokens,\n120\t    }\n121\t}\n122\t\n123\t/// Resolve a dot-separated path in a JSON value (e.g., `result.usage.input_tokens`).\n124\tfn resolve_json_path<'a>(\n125\t    value: &'a serde_json::Value,\n126\t    path: &str,\n127\t) -> Option<&'a serde_json::Value> {\n128\t    let mut current = value;\n129\t    for key in path.split('.') {\n130\t        current = current.get(key)?;\n131\t    }\n132\t    Some(current)\n133\t}\n134\t\n135\t/// Extract tokens from text using a regex with numbered capture groups.\n136\tfn extract_tokens_regex(\n137\t    text: &str,\n138\t    pattern: &str,\n139\t    input_group: usize,\n140\t    output_group: usize,\n141\t) -> TokenUsage {\n142\t    let re = match Regex::new(pattern) {\n143\t        Ok(r) => r,\n144\t        Err(_) => return TokenUsage::default(),\n145\t    };\n146\t\n147\t    let caps = match re.captures(text) {\n148\t        Some(c) => c,\n149\t        None => return TokenUsage::default(),\n150\t    };\n151\t\n152\t    let parse_group = |group: usize| -> Option<u64> {\n153\t        caps.get(group)?\n154\t            .as_str()\n155\t            .replace(',', \"\")\n156\t            .parse::<u64>()\n157\t            .ok()\n158\t    };\n159\t\n160\t    TokenUsage {\n161\t        input_tokens: parse_group(input_group),\n162\t        output_tokens: parse_group(output_group),\n163\t    }\n164\t}\n165\t\n166\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n167\t// AgentAdapter\n168\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n169\t\n170\t/// Configuration for a single agent adapter, loaded from YAML or embedded.\n171\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, InputMethod::Stdin\n224\t}\n225\t\n226\timpl AgentAdapter {\n227\t    /// Effective timeout as a `Duration`, falling back to the global config.\n228\t    pub fn effective_timeout(&self,   };\n234\t        if secs == 0 {\n235\t            Duration::ZERO\n236\t        } else {\n237\t            Duration::from_secs(secs)\n238\t        }\n239\t    }\n240\t\n241\t    /// Returns the GenAI ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n408\t\n409\t/// Substitute known variables in an invoke template.\n410\tfn render_template(\n411\t    template: &str,\n412\t    workspace: &Path,\n413\t    prompt_file: &Path,\n414\t    bead_id: &BeadId,\n415\t    model: &str,\n416\t) -> String {\n417\t    template\n418\t        .replace(\"{workspace}\", .replace(\"{model}\", model)\n422\t}\n423\t\n424\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n425\t// Adapter loading\n426\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n427\t\n428\t/// Load adapters from YAML files, with built-in defaults.\n429\t///\n430\t/// Built-in adapters are loaded first; user files in `dir` override by name.\n431\tpub fn load_adapters(\n432\t    dir: &Path,\n433\t    built_ins: &[AgentAdapter],\n434\t) -> Result<HashMap<String, AgentAdapter>> {\n435\t    let mut adapters = HashMap::new();\n436\t\n437\t    for adapter in built_ins {\n438\t        adapters.insert(adapter.name.clone(), adapter.clone());\n439\t    }\n440\t\n441\t    if dir.exists() && dir.is_dir() {\n442\t        let entries = std::fs::read_dir(dir)\n443\t            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to read adapters dir: {}\", dir.display()))?;\n444\t\n445\t        for entry in entries {\n446\t            let entry = entry?;\n447\t            let path = entry.path();\n448\t            let is_yaml = path\n449\t                .extension()\n450\t                .is_some_and(|ext| ext == \"yaml\" || ext == \"yml\");\n451\t            if is_yaml {\n452\t                let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path)\n453\t                    .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to read adapter file: {}\", path.display()))?;\n454\t                let adapter: AgentAdapter = serde_yaml::from_str(&text)\n455\t                    .with_context(|| format!(\"invalid YAML in adapter file: {}\", path.display()))?;\n456\t                adapters.insert(adapter.name.clone(), adapter);\n457\t            }\n458\t        }\n459\t    }\n460\t\n461\t    Ok(adapters)\n462\t}\n463\t\n464\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n465\t// Dispatcher\n466\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n467\t\n468\t/// Executes agent processes for claimed beads.\n469\tpub struct Dispatcher {\n470\t    adapters: HashMap<String, AgentAdapter>,\n471\t    telemetry: Telemetry,\n472\t    global_timeout_secs: u64,\n473\t    /// Sanitizer applied to all trace content before writing to disk.\n474\t    /// `None` config.\n480\t    pub fn new(config: &Config, telemetry: Telemetry) -> Result<Self> {\n481\t        let adapters = load_adapters(&config.agent.adapters_dir, &builtin_adapters())?;\n482\t        let sanitizer = build_sanitizer(config);\n483\t        Ok(Dispatcher {\n484\t            adapters,\n485\t            telemetry,\n486\t            global_timeout_secs: config.agent.timeout,\n487\t            sanitizer,\n488\t        })\n489\t    }\n490\t\n491\t    /// Create a dispatcher with explicit adapters (for testing).\n492\t    pub fn with_adapters(\n493\t        adapters: HashMap<String, AgentAdapter>,\n494\t        telemetry: Telemetry,\n495\t        global_timeout_secs: u64,\n496\t    ) -> Self {\n497\t        Dispatcher {\n498\t            adapters,\n499\t            telemetry,\n500\t            global_timeout_secs,\n501\t            sanitizer: None,\n502\t        }\n503\t    }\n504\t\n505\t    /// Look up an adapter by name.\n506\t    pub fn adapter(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&AgentAdapter> {\n507\t        self.adapters.get(name)\n508\t    }\n509\t\n510\t    /// List all loaded adapter names.\n511\t    pub fn adapter_names(&self) -> Vec<&str> {\n512\t        self.adapters.keys().map(|s| s.as_str()).collect()\n513\t    }\n514\t\n515\t    /// Resolve an adapter name from a model name using routing rules.\n516\t    ///\n517\t    /// If routing is configured in the config, returns the adapter name that\n518\t    /// matches the model pattern (first match wins). Otherwise, returns the\n519\t    /// default adapter name.\n520\t    ///\n521\t    /// # Arguments\n522\t    /// * `model` - Model name (e.g., \"sonnet\", \"claude-opus-4-8\", \"fable\")\n523\t    /// * `config` - Full config containing routing rules\n524\t    ///\n525\t    /// # Returns\n526\t    /// The resolved adapter name (e.g., \"claude-code-glm-4.7\", \"claude-sonnet\")\n527\t    pub fn resolve_adapter_name(&self, model: &str, config: &Config) -> String {\n528\t        if let Some(ref routing) = config.agent.routing {\n529\t            // Check each routing rule in order (first match wins)\n530\t            for rule in &routing.rules {\n531\t                match regex::Regex::new(&rule.match_model) {\n532\t                    Ok(re) => {\n533\t                        if re.is_match(model) {\n534\t                            tracing::debug!(\n535\t                                model = %model,\n536\t                                pattern = %rule.match_model,\n537\t                                adapter = %rule.adapter,\n538\t                                \"routing rule matched\"\n539\t                            );\n540\t                            return rule.adapter.clone();\n541\t                        }\n542\t                    }\n543\t                    Err(_) => {\n544\t                        // Regex was validated during config load, so this should never happen.\n545\t                        // Fall back to default if somehow we get an invalid pattern at runtime.\n546\t                        tracing::warn!(\n547\t                            pattern = %rule.match_model,\n548\t                            \"invalid routing regex pattern (should have been caught by config validation)\"\n549\t                        );\n550\t                        continue;\n551\t                    }\n552\t                }\n553\t            }\n554\t\n555\t            // No rules matched; use default_adapter if set\n556\t            if let Some(ref default_adapter) = routing.default_adapter {\n557\t                tracing::debug!(\n558\t                    model = %model,\n559\t                    adapter = %default_adapter,\n560\t                    \"no routing rules matched, using default_adapter\"\n561\t                );\n562\t                return default_adapter.clone();\n563\t            }\n564\t        }\n565\t\n566\t        // No routing configured or no default_adapter; fall back to agent.default\n567\t        tracing::debug!(\n568\t            model = %model,\n569\t            adapter = %config.agent.default,\n570\t            \"no routing configured, using agent.default\"\n571\t        );\n572\t        config.agent.default.clone()\n573\t    }\n574\t\n575\t    /// Execute the agent process for a bead.\n576\t    ///\n577\t    /// 1. Writes the prompt to a temp file\n578\t    /// 2. Renders the invoke template with variables\n579\t    /// 3. Sets adapter-specific environment variables\n580\t    /// 4. Spawns the process via `bash -c`\n581\t    /// 5. Waits with timeout enforcement (kills on timeout, exit 124)\n582\t    /// 6. Captures stdout, stderr, exit code\n583\t    /// 7. Cleans up the temp file\n584\t    #[tracing::instrument(\n585\t        name = \"agent.dispatch\",\n586\t        skip(self, bead_id, prompt, adapter, workspace),\n587\t        fields(\n588\t            needle.bead.id = %bead_id.as_ref(),\n589\t            gen_ai.system = %adapter.gen_ai_system(),\n590\t            gen_ai.operation.name = \"chat\",\n591\t            gen_ai.request.id = %bead_id.as_ref(),\n592\t            needle.agent.pid = tracing::field::Empty,\n593\t            needle.agent.exit_code = tracing::field::Empty,\n594\t        )\n595\t    )]\n596\t    pub async fn dispatch(\n597\t        &self,\n598\t        bead_id: &BeadId,\n599\t        prompt: &BuiltPrompt,\n600\t        adapter: &AgentAdapter,\n601\t        workspace: &Path,\n602\t    ) -> Result<ExecutionResult> {\n603\t        // Set gen_ai.request.model if available\n604\t        if let Some(ref model) = adapter.model {\n605\t            tracing::Span::current().record(\"gen_ai.request.model\", model.as_str());\n606\t        }\n607\t\n608\t        self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::DispatchStarted {\n609\t            bead_id:           }\n848\t            }\n849\t        } else {\n850\t            let _ = self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::TransformSkipped {\n851\t                bead_id: bead_id.clone(),\n852\t }\n1031\t}\n1032\t\n1033\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n1034\t// Helpers\n1035\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n1036\t\n1037\t/// Compute the per-bead agent log path: `~/.needle/logs/<worker>-<bead_id>.agent.jsonl`.\n1038\t///\n1039\t/// Build a trace sanitizer from the workspace config.\n1040\t///\n1041\t/// Returns `None` if sanitization is disabled or if rule
